### 2026-11-03 — S3-07/V1-journal-primary-metrics

Implemented backend and active-owner CLI `journal:v1 create|get|export`. Policy last_successful_pre_start_v1 selects one eligible forecast per actual verified map by full-microsecond issuedAt, createdAt, then canonical UUID. Global selection precedes model/time/status filters and matched-lineup analysis; filters never promote an earlier forecast. Period explicitly means forecast_created_at, [fromAt,until). Mismatched/unknown actual rosters remain in overall performance. Frozen issuance gate, not current model activation, determines historical eligibility. What-if, unavailable, pending, unlinked, excluded and superseded attempts remain auditable.

Pure forecast-binary-v1 metrics use unrounded probability: accuracy with separate accuracyN/ties (exact p=.5 has no favourite), Brier, natural log loss clipped at1e-15 and ten fixed Team-A reliability bins. Empty sample/bin rates are null. Matched-lineup metrics are a separate slice of already selected primary forecasts. Cancellation lifecycle remains unimplemented: cancelled is explicitly null with a warning, not a claimed zero count. Journal statistics do not train or activate models.

Capture reads the complete owner V1 inventory in one repeatable-read transaction. An existing immutable verified binding proposes the same actual map for every peer with exactly the same source-series/map selector; every peer is freshly reverified. This includes the latest recalculation even when only an earlier forecast was individually refreshed. Conflicting map bindings remain unlinked. Capture appends no outcome revisions and guesses no new target IDs. Source corrections create new report versions; prior report bytes replay unchanged. Full bounded projection, snapshot/outcome bindings, fresh verification, code fingerprint, input/report SHA and owner revision/previous-SHA chain are stored in new SQL028. Session advisory lock precedes RR snapshot acquisition for concurrent retries; SQL checks complete inventory, source fields and current verification. UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE are refused. Limits5000 observations/32MiB per document fail closed instead of truncating. Same requestId/body reuses its old report; changed request conflicts. Export uses exclusive file creation and stays local/ignored.
